Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on extremal structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 225. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 363 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to extremal theory, we decompose the problem into 225 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 363. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
